  16    class WhatsAppNotificationListener : NotificationListenerService() {
  17    
  18        companion object {
  19            private const val TAG = "JARVIS-NOTIF"
  20            private const val WHATSAPP = "com.whatsapp"
  21    
  22            private const val NORMAL_COOLDOWN_MS = 30_000L
  23            private const val URGENT_COOLDOWN_MS = 5 * 60 * 1000L
  24    
  25            private val URGENT_REGEX = Regex(
  26                "\\b(urgent|emergency|asap|important|immediately|call\\s*me|call\\s*now)\\b",
  27                RegexOption.IGNORE_CASE
  28            )
  29        }
  30    
  31        private val handledKeys = ConcurrentHashMap<String, Boolean>()
  32        private val lastReplyTime = ConcurrentHashMap<String, Long>()
  33        private val lastUrgentReplyTime = ConcurrentHashMap<String, Long>()
  34    
  35        override fun onNotificationPosted(sbn: StatusBarNotification?) {
  36            if (sbn == null) return
  37            if (sbn.packageName != WHATSAPP) return
  38    
  39            val notification = sbn.notification
  40            val extras = notification.extras
  41    
  42            if (notification.flags and Notification.FLAG_GROUP_SUMMARY != 0) return
  43    
  44            val title = extras.getString(Notification.EXTRA_TITLE)
  45            val convo = extras.getString(Notification.EXTRA_CONVERSATION_TITLE)
  46    
  47            val isGroup =
  48                extras.getBoolean(Notification.EXTRA_IS_GROUP_CONVERSATION, false) ||
  49                (!title.isNullOrEmpty() && !convo.isNullOrEmpty() && title != convo)
  50    
  51            if (isGroup) return
  52    
  53            val replyAction = notification.actions
  54                ?.firstOrNull { it.remoteInputs != null }
  55                ?: return
  56    
  57            if (handledKeys.putIfAbsent(sbn.key, true) != null) return
  58    
  59            val chatId = convo ?: title ?: return
  60            val now = System.currentTimeMillis()
  61    
  62            val messageText =
  63                extras.getCharSequence(Notification.EXTRA_TEXT)?.toString()
  64                    ?: extras.getCharSequence(Notification.EXTRA_BIG_TEXT)?.toString()
  65                    ?: ""
  66    
  67            val isUrgent = URGENT_REGEX.containsMatchIn(messageText)
  68    
  69            if (isUrgent) {
  70                val last = lastUrgentReplyTime[chatId] ?: 0L
  71                if (now - last < URGENT_COOLDOWN_MS) return
  72                lastUrgentReplyTime[chatId] = now
  73            } else {
  74                val last = lastReplyTime[chatId] ?: 0L
  75                if (now - last < NORMAL_COOLDOWN_MS) return
  76                lastReplyTime[chatId] = now
  77            }
  78    
  79 ❌         val decision = AutoReplyOrchestrator.handle(
  80 ❌             AutoReplyInput(
  81                    messageText = messageText,
  82                    isFromOwner = false
  83                )
  84            )
  85    
  86 ❌         if (decision !is ReplyDecision.AutoReply) {
  87                Log.d(TAG, "Jarvis mode blocks auto-reply")
  88                return
  89            }
  90    
  91            sendReply(replyAction, decision.message)
  92        }
  93    
  94        private fun sendReply(action: Notification.Action, replyText: String) {
  95            val fillInIntent = Intent()
  96            val results = Bundle()
  97    
  98            for (input in action.remoteInputs) {
  99                results.putCharSequence(input.resultKey, replyText)
 100            }
 101    
 102            RemoteInput.addResultsToIntent(
 103                action.remoteInputs,
 104                fillInIntent,
 105                results
 106            )
 107    
 108            try {
 109                action.actionIntent.send(
 110                    this,
 111                    0,
 112                    fillInIntent
 113                )
 114                Log.d(TAG, "Jarvis auto-reply sent (stable)")
 115            } catch (e: PendingIntent.CanceledException) {
 116                Log.e(TAG, "Failed to send reply", e)
 117            }
 118        }
 119    }

 188 ❌ @Composable
 189    private fun StatusBar(state: AgentDashboardState) {
 190        val bg = when {
 191            state.error != null -> Red.copy(alpha = 0.15f)
 192            state.done          -> Green.copy(alpha = 0.12f)
 193            state.running       -> Cyan.copy(alpha = 0.08f)
 194            else                -> BgCard
 195        }
 196        val text = when {
 197            state.error != null -> "✗ Error: ${state.error}"
 198            state.done          -> "✓ Done in ${state.step} steps"
 199            state.running       -> "Step ${state.step}  —  ${state.task.take(50)}"
 200            else                -> ""
 201        }
 202        val textColor = when {
 203            state.error != null -> Red
 204            state.done          -> Green
 205            else                -> Cyan
 206        }
 207        if (text.isBlank()) return
 208 ❌     Row(
 209 ❌         Modifier
 210                .fillMaxWidth()
 211 ❌             .clip(RoundedCornerShape(6.dp))
 212                .background(bg)
 213 ❌             .padding(horizontal = 10.dp, vertical = 6.dp),
 214 ❌         verticalAlignment = Alignment.CenterVertically
 215        ) {
 216            if (state.running) {
 217 ❌             CircularProgressIndicator(
 218 ❌                 modifier = Modifier.size(12.dp),
 219                    color    = Cyan,
 220 ❌                 strokeWidth = 1.5.dp
 221                )
 222 ❌             Spacer(Modifier.width(8.dp))
 223            }
 224 ❌         Text(
 225                text,
 226                color    = textColor,
 227 ❌             fontSize = 12.sp,
 228 ❌             fontFamily = FontFamily.Monospace,
 229                maxLines = 1
 230            )
 231        }
 232    }

 330 ❌ @Composable
 331    private fun LogLineRow(log: LogLine) {
 332        val color = when (log.level) {
 333            LogLevel.SUCCESS -> Green
 334            LogLevel.ERROR   -> Red
 335            LogLevel.WARN    -> Yellow
 336            LogLevel.STEP    -> Cyan
 337            LogLevel.SYSTEM  -> DimCyan
 338            LogLevel.INFO    -> White70
 339        }
 340 ❌     Text(
 341            text       = log.text,
 342            color      = color,
 343 ❌         fontSize   = 11.sp,
 344 ❌         fontFamily = FontFamily.Monospace,
 345 ❌         lineHeight = 14.sp,
 346 ❌         modifier   = Modifier
 347                .fillMaxWidth()
 348 ❌             .padding(vertical = 1.dp, horizontal = 2.dp)
 349        )
 350    }

 354 ❌ @Composable
 355    private fun ServerStatusDot(online: Boolean, running: Boolean) {
 356 ❌     val pulse = rememberInfiniteTransition(label = "pulse")
 357        val alpha by pulse.animateFloat(
 358            initialValue = 0.4f, targetValue = 1f,
 359 ❌         animationSpec = infiniteRepeatable(
 360 ❌             animation  = tween(900, easing = FastOutSlowInEasing),
 361 ❌             repeatMode = RepeatMode.Reverse
 362            ),
 363            label = "dotAlpha"
 364        )
 365        val color = when {
 366            !online  -> Red
 367            running  -> Green.copy(alpha = alpha)
 368            else     -> Green
 369        }
 370 ❌     Box(
 371 ❌         Modifier
 372 ❌             .size(10.dp)
 373 ❌             .clip(CircleShape)
 374                .background(color)
 375        )
 376    }

  20        val logs:          List<LogLine>  = emptyList(),
  21        val taskInput:     String         = "",
  22        val deviceInput:   String         = "192.168.29.48:40657",
  23        val maxSteps:      Int            = 20,
  24        val ttsEnabled:    Boolean        = true,
  25    )
  26    
  27    data class LogLine(
  28        val text:  String,
  29        val level: LogLevel = LogLevel.INFO,
  30    )
  31    
  32    enum class LogLevel { INFO, STEP, SUCCESS, ERROR, WARN, SYSTEM }
  33    
  34    fun classifyLog(line: String): LogLevel = when {
  35        line.contains("✓") || line.contains("Task complete") || line.contains("completed") -> LogLevel.SUCCESS
  36        line.contains("✗") || line.contains("ERROR") || line.contains("error:", ignoreCase = true) -> LogLevel.ERROR
  37        line.contains("WARNING") || line.contains("WARN") || line.contains("Retry") -> LogLevel.WARN
  38        line.contains("STEP") && line.contains("|") -> LogLevel.STEP
  39        line.startsWith("[server]") || line.startsWith("[agent_server]") -> LogLevel.SYSTEM
  40        else -> LogLevel.INFO
  41    }
  42    
  43    class AgentDashboardViewModel : ViewModel() {
  44

  46        val state: StateFlow<AgentDashboardState> = _state.asStateFlow()

  85            }
  86        }
  87

  92            }
  93        }
  94    
  95        fun clearLogs() = _state.update { it.copy(logs = emptyList()) }
  96    
  97        // Manual "Speak" button — speaks last SUCCESS/STEP log line
  98        fun speakResult(context: Context? = null) {
  99            val last = _state.value.logs
 100                .filter { it.level == LogLevel.SUCCESS || it.level == LogLevel.STEP }
 101                .lastOrNull()?.text ?: return
 102    
 103            val voiceId = resolveVoiceId()
 104 ❌         viewModelScope.launch {
 105 ❌             AgentRepository.speak(last, voiceId)
 106            }
 107        }
 108    
 109        // ── Auto-speak watcher ────────────────────────────────────────────────────
 110        // Fires automatically when the agent finishes (done flips true) and TTS is on.
 111        // Speaks the last SUCCESS line so Sir hears the result hands-free.
 112        private fun observeDoneForAutoSpeak() {
 113            autoSpeakJob?.cancel()
 114 ❌         autoSpeakJob = viewModelScope.launch {
 115                var prevDone = false
 116 ❌             state.collect { s ->
 117                    if (s.done && !prevDone && s.ttsEnabled) {
 118                        // Task just completed — find best line to speak
 119                        val toSpeak = s.logs
 120                            .filter { it.level == LogLevel.SUCCESS }
 121                            .lastOrNull()?.text
 122                            ?: s.logs.lastOrNull()?.text
 123                        if (!toSpeak.isNullOrBlank()) {
 124                            val voiceId = resolveVoiceId()
 125                            AgentRepository.speak(toSpeak, voiceId)
 126                        }
 127                    }
 128                    prevDone = s.done
 129                }
 130            }
 131        }
 132    
 133        // Reads the voice ID that was saved in Settings (elevenlabs_voice_id pref).
 134        // Falls back to the Jarvis custom voice if not set.
 135        private fun resolveVoiceId(): String {
 136 ❌         val saved = JarvisPrefs.getString("elevenlabs_voice_id")

 138        }
 139

 142 ❌         logStreamJob = viewModelScope.launch {
 143 ❌             AgentRepository.streamLogs().collect { line ->
 144                    pushLog(line, classifyLog(line))
 145                }
 146            }
 147        }
 148    
 149        private fun pollStatus() {
 150            statusPollJob?.cancel()
 151 ❌         statusPollJob = viewModelScope.launch {
 152                while (true) {
 153 ❌                 delay(2000)
 154 ❌                 val status = AgentRepository.getStatus()

 156                        running      = status.running,
 157                        task         = status.task,
 158                        step         = status.step,
 159                        done         = status.done,
 160                        error        = status.error,
 161                        serverOnline = true,
 162                    )}
 163                }
 164            }
 165        }
 166    
 167        private fun pushLog(text: String, level: LogLevel = LogLevel.INFO) {
 168            _state.update { s ->
 169                val logs = (s.logs + LogLine(text, level)).takeLast(200)
 170                s.copy(logs = logs)
 171            }
 172        }
 173    
 174        override fun onCleared() {
 175            logStreamJob?.cancel()
 176            statusPollJob?.cancel()
 177            autoSpeakJob?.cancel()
 178            super.onCleared()
 179        }
 180    }

  69            pairingEnabled = true
  70            pairingExpiry = System.currentTimeMillis() + (durationSeconds * 1000)
  71            Log.i(tag, "🔓 Pairing mode ENABLED for $durationSeconds seconds (Instance: ${this.hashCode()})")
  72            Log.i(tag, "   Expiry timestamp: $pairingExpiry")
  73        }

  79        }
  80    
  81        fun isPairingEnabled(): Boolean {
  82            val now = System.currentTimeMillis()
  83            val remaining = (pairingExpiry - now) / 1000
  84            
  85            Log.d(tag, "🔍 Pairing check (Instance: ${this.hashCode()}):")
  86            Log.d(tag, "   pairingEnabled: $pairingEnabled")
  87            Log.d(tag, "   Expiry: $pairingExpiry")
  88            Log.d(tag, "   Now: $now")
  89            Log.d(tag, "   Remaining: ${remaining}s")
  90            
  91            if (!pairingEnabled) {
  92                Log.w(tag, "❌ Pairing DENIED: pairingEnabled is false")
  93                return false
  94            }
  95            
  96            if (now > pairingExpiry) {
  97                Log.w(tag, "❌ Pairing DENIED: Expired ${-remaining}s ago")
  98                disablePairingMode()
  99                return false
 100            }
 101            
 102            Log.i(tag, "✅ Pairing ALLOWED: ${remaining}s remaining")
 103            return true
 104        }

 108        fun pairDevice(deviceName: String, ipAddress: String): PairResult {
 109            Log.i(tag, "📱 pairDevice called (Instance: ${this.hashCode()}):")
 110            Log.i(tag, "   Device name: '$deviceName'")
 111            Log.i(tag, "   IP address: '$ipAddress'")
 112            
 113            if (!isPairingEnabled()) {
 114                Log.e(tag, "❌ Pairing REJECTED: Pairing mode is disabled")
 115                return PairResult(false, error = "Pairing mode disabled.")
 116            }
 117    
 118            val token = generateSecureToken()
 119            val tokenHash = hashToken(token)
 120            val deviceId = generateDeviceId()
 121    
 122            val device = TrustedDevice(
 123                id = deviceId,
 124                name = deviceName.take(100),
 125                tokenHash = tokenHash,
 126                ipAddress = ipAddress,
 127                pairedAt = System.currentTimeMillis(),
 128                lastSeen = System.currentTimeMillis()
 129            )
 130    
 131            deviceRegistry[deviceId] = device
 132            tokenIndex[tokenHash] = deviceId
 133            saveDevices()
 134    
 135            Log.i(tag, "✅ Device PAIRED successfully:")
 136            Log.i(tag, "   Device ID: $deviceId")
 137            Log.i(tag, "   Name: $deviceName")
 138            Log.i(tag, "   Token (first 16 chars): ${token.take(16)}...")
 139            
 140            return PairResult(true, deviceId, token)
 141        }
 142    
 143        fun unpairDevice(deviceId: String): Boolean {
 144            val device = deviceRegistry.remove(deviceId) ?: return false
 145            tokenIndex.remove(device.tokenHash)
 146            rateLimitMap.remove(deviceId)
 147            saveDevices()
 148            Log.i(tag, "Device unpaired: $deviceId")

 152        fun getPairedDevices(): List<TrustedDevice> =

 155        fun authenticateToken(auth: AuthContext): AuthResult {
 156            val token = auth.deviceToken ?: return AuthResult.failure("Missing token")
 157            val tokenHash = hashToken(token)
 158            val deviceId = tokenIndex[tokenHash] ?: return AuthResult.failure("Invalid token")
 159            val device = deviceRegistry[deviceId] ?: return AuthResult.failure("Device not found")
 160    
 161            device.lastSeen = System.currentTimeMillis()
 162            return AuthResult.success(device)
 163        }
 164    
 165        fun checkRateLimit(deviceId: String, endpoint: String): Boolean {
 166            val now = System.currentTimeMillis()
 167            val deviceLimits = rateLimitMap.getOrPut(deviceId) { ConcurrentHashMap() }
 168            val timestamps = deviceLimits.getOrPut(endpoint) { mutableListOf() }
 169    
 170            synchronized(timestamps) {
 171                timestamps.removeAll { it < now - 60000 }
 172    
 173                val limit = when (endpoint) {
 174                    "/generate", "/chat" -> 20
 175                    "/execute" -> 60
 176                    else -> 100
 177                }
 178    
 179                return if (timestamps.size < limit) {
 180                    timestamps.add(now)
 181                    true
 182                } else {
 183                    false
 184                }
 185            }
 186        }
 187    
 188        fun isCommandAllowed(command: String): CommandValidation {
 189            val trimmed = command.trim()
 190    
 191            if (trimmed.isEmpty()) {
 192                return CommandValidation(false, "Empty command")
 193            }
 194    
 195            val firstToken = trimmed.split(" ").firstOrNull() ?: ""
 196    
 197            if (firstToken in allowedCommands) {
 198                return CommandValidation(true, null)
 199            }
 200    
 201            val dangerous = listOf("rm", "dd", "mkfs", "format", ">", "curl", "wget")
 202            if (dangerous.any { trimmed.contains(it) }) {
 203                return CommandValidation(false, "Dangerous command blocked")
 204            }
 205    
 206            return CommandValidation(true, null)
 207        }
 208    
 209        private fun generateSecureToken(): String {
 210            val random = SecureRandom()
 211            val bytes = ByteArray(32)
 212            random.nextBytes(bytes)
 213            return bytes.joinToString("") { "%02x".format(it) }
 214        }
 215    
 216        private fun generateDeviceId(): String {
 217            return "dev_${System.currentTimeMillis()}_${(1000..9999).random()}"
 218        }
 219    
 220        private fun hashToken(token: String): String {
 221            val digest = MessageDigest.getInstance("SHA-256")
 222            return digest.digest(token.toByteArray())
 223                .joinToString("") { "%02x".format(it) }
 224        }
 225    
 226        private fun saveDevices() {
 227            val jsonString = json.encodeToString(deviceRegistry.values.toList())
 228            prefs.edit().putString("devices", jsonString).apply()
 229        }
 230    
 231        private fun loadDevices() {
 232            val jsonString = prefs.getString("devices", null) ?: return
 233            try {
 234                val deviceList = json.decodeFromString<List<TrustedDevice>>(jsonString)
 235 ❌             deviceList.forEach {
 236 ❌                 deviceRegistry[it.id] = it
 237 ❌                 tokenIndex[it.tokenHash] = it.id
 238                }
 239            } catch (_: Exception) {}
 240        }
 241    }

 108        private fun normalizeEndpoint(uri: String): String {
 109            // Remove /api/v1 prefix if present
 110            var normalized = uri
 111            if (normalized.startsWith("/api/v1/")) {
 112                normalized = normalized.removePrefix("/api/v1")
 113            } else if (normalized.startsWith("/api/")) {
 114                normalized = normalized.removePrefix("/api")
 115            }
 116            
 117            // Ensure it starts with /
 118            if (!normalized.startsWith("/")) {
 119                normalized = "/$normalized"
 120            }

 110 ❌     private fun updateAvailableModels(status: ModelStatus) {

 126    
 127    private fun getCurrentDayOfWeek(): String {
 128        val cal = Calendar.getInstance()
 129        return when (cal.get(Calendar.DAY_OF_WEEK)) {
 130            Calendar.SUNDAY -> "SUN"
 131            Calendar.MONDAY -> "MON"
 132            Calendar.TUESDAY -> "TUE"
 133            Calendar.WEDNESDAY -> "WED"
 134            Calendar.THURSDAY -> "THU"
 135            Calendar.FRIDAY -> "FRI"
 136            Calendar.SATURDAY -> "SAT"
 137            else -> "SUN"
 138        }
 139    }
 140    
 141    private fun parseTimeToMs(timeStr: String): Long {
 142        return try {
 143            val cal = Calendar.getInstance()
 144            val parts = timeStr.split(":")
 145            cal.set(Calendar.HOUR_OF_DAY, parts[0].toInt())
 146            cal.set(Calendar.MINUTE, parts[1].toInt())
 147            cal.set(Calendar.SECOND, 0)
 148            cal.set(Calendar.MILLISECOND, 0)
 149            cal.timeInMillis
 150        } catch (_: Exception) {
 151            System.currentTimeMillis()
 152        }
 153    }

   7 ❌     EventStatus.SCHEDULED -> Color(0xFF64B5F6)      // Blue
   8 ❌     EventStatus.COMPLETED -> Color(0xFF81C784)      // Green
   9 ❌     EventStatus.INCOMPLETE -> Color(0xFFE57373)     // Red ✅ fixed
  10 ❌     EventStatus.RETRY_SCHEDULED -> Color(0xFFFFB74D)// Orange ✅ fixed
  11 ❌     else -> Color.Gray                               // ✅ exhaustive 'when'
  12    }

 311                    }
 312                }
 313            }

 129 ❌                         Text("DONE", fontFamily = FontFamily.Monospace, fontSize = 12.sp)
 130                        }

 133 ❌                         Text("MISSED", fontFamily = FontFamily.Monospace, fontSize = 12.sp)

 149    private fun formatTime(timestampMs: Long): String {
 150        val formatter = SimpleDateFormat("h:mm a", Locale.getDefault())
 151        return formatter.format(Date(timestampMs))

 319    // ── Helpers ──────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────
 320    
 321    /**
 322     * FIX: Sort key for display order respecting 3 AM boundary.
 323     * Times 00:00–02:59 get +1440 so they appear AFTER 23:59.
 324     * Result: 08:00(480) → 19:00(1140) → 23:30(1410) → 00:20(1460) → 00:50(1490) → 01:00(1500)
 325     */
 326    private fun parseTimeToMinutesOrdered(time: String): Int {
 327        return try {
 328            val parts   = time.split(":")
 329            val hours   = parts[0].toInt()
 330            val minutes = parts[1].toInt()
 331            val raw     = hours * 60 + minutes
 332            // Anything before 03:00 belongs to the tail end of the previous evening
 333            if (hours < 3) raw + 1440 else raw
 334        } catch (_: Exception) {
 335            0
 336        }
 337    }
 338    
 339    /**
 340     * FIX: day-of-week respects 3 AM boundary.
 341     * Before 03:00, we're still in "yesterday's" session.
 342     */
 343    private fun getCurrentDayOfWeek(): String {
 344        val cal = Calendar.getInstance()
 345        if (cal.get(Calendar.HOUR_OF_DAY) < 3) {
 346            cal.add(Calendar.DAY_OF_YEAR, -1)
 347        }
 348        return when (cal.get(Calendar.DAY_OF_WEEK)) {
 349            Calendar.SUNDAY    -> "SUN"
 350            Calendar.MONDAY    -> "MON"
 351            Calendar.TUESDAY   -> "TUE"
 352            Calendar.WEDNESDAY -> "WED"
 353            Calendar.THURSDAY  -> "THU"
 354            Calendar.FRIDAY    -> "FRI"
 355            Calendar.SATURDAY  -> "SAT"
 356            else               -> "SUN"
 357        }
 358    }

  12    import kotlin.math.cos
  13    import kotlin.math.sin
  14    import kotlin.math.PI
  15    
  16 ❌ @Composable
  17    fun HologramCanvas() {
  18 ❌     val infiniteTransition = rememberInfiniteTransition(label = "hologram")
  19        val rotation by infiniteTransition.animateFloat(
  20            initialValue = 0f,
  21            targetValue = 360f,
  22 ❌         animationSpec = infiniteRepeatable(
  23 ❌             animation = tween(10000, easing = LinearEasing),
  24 ❌             repeatMode = RepeatMode.Restart
  25            ),
  26            label = "rotation"
  27        )
  28    
  29 ❌     Canvas(modifier = Modifier.fillMaxSize()) {
  30 ❌         val center = Offset(size.width / 2f, size.height / 2f)
  31 ❌         val radius = size.minDimension * 0.3f
  32    
  33            // Draw rotating hexagon
  34 ❌         val path = Path()
  35            for (i in 0..6) {
  36                val angle = (i / 6f) * 2 * PI + rotation * PI / 180f
  37                val x = center.x + radius * cos(angle).toFloat()
  38                val y = center.y + radius * sin(angle).toFloat()
  39    
  40                if (i == 0) {
  41                    path.moveTo(x, y)
  42                } else {
  43                    path.lineTo(x, y)
  44                }
  45            }
  46    
  47 ❌         drawPath(
  48                path = path,
  49 ❌             color = Color(0xFF00E0FF).copy(alpha = 0.6f),
  50 ❌             style = Stroke(width = 2f)
  51            )
  52        }
  53    }

  51

  53 ❌     val pulseAnimation = rememberInfiniteTransition(label = "pulse")

 252        onClick: () -> Unit,
 253    ) {

 261 ❌         Box(

 264 ❌                 .background(Color.Black.copy(alpha = 0.5f))

 266 ❌                     width = 1.dp,
 267 ❌                     color = Color(0xFF00E0FF).copy(alpha = 0.4f),
 268 ❌                     shape = RoundedCornerShape(12.dp)
 269                    ),
 270 ❌             contentAlignment = Alignment.Center
 271            ) {
 272 ❌             Column(
 273 ❌                 horizontalAlignment = Alignment.CenterHorizontally,
 274 ❌                 verticalArrangement = Arrangement.Center
 275                ) {
 276 ❌                 Icon(
 277                        imageVector        = icon,

 279 ❌                     tint               = Color(0xFF00E0FF),

 281                    )
 282 ❌                 Spacer(Modifier.height(8.dp))

  25    /**
  26     * Main screen for Termux command generation
  27     * 
  28     * ✅ FIXED: ViewModel moved to separate file
  29     * Now uses FIFO for all command execution (no more Termux intent)
  30     */
  31 ❌ @OptIn(ExperimentalMaterial3Api::class)
  32 ❌ @Composable
  33    fun TermuxCommandScreen(
  34        onNavigateBack: () -> Unit = {}
  35    ) {
  36 ❌     val context = LocalContext.current
  37 ❌     val viewModel = remember { TermuxCommandViewModel(context) }
  38        
  39        val uiState by viewModel.uiState.collectAsState()
  40        val serverStatus by viewModel.serverStatus.collectAsState()
  41        val commandHistory by viewModel.commandHistory.collectAsState()
  42        
  43 ❌     var currentQuery by remember { mutableStateOf("") }
  44 ❌     var showHistory by remember { mutableStateOf(false) }
  45        
  46 ❌     Scaffold(
  47            topBar = {
  48 ❌             TopAppBar(
  49 ❌                 title = { Text("Termux Command Generator") },
  50                    navigationIcon = {
  51 ❌                     IconButton(onClick = onNavigateBack) {
  52 ❌                         Icon(Icons.Default.ArrowBack, "Back")
  53                        }
  54                    },
  55                    actions = {
  56                        ServerStatusChip(
  57                            status = serverStatus,
  58                            onRefresh = { viewModel.checkServerStatus() }
  59                        )
  60                        
  61 ❌                     IconButton(onClick = { showHistory = !showHistory }) {
  62 ❌                         Icon(
  63 ❌                             if (showHistory) Icons.Default.Clear else Icons.Default.History,
  64                                "Command History"
  65                            )
  66                        }
  67                    },
  68 ❌                 colors = TopAppBarDefaults.topAppBarColors(
  69                        containerColor = JarvisBlue.copy(alpha = 0.1f)
  70                    )
  71                )
  72            }
  73 ❌     ) { padding ->
  74 ❌         Box(modifier = Modifier.fillMaxSize()) {
  75 ❌             AnimatedVisibility(
  76                    visible = !showHistory,
  77 ❌                 enter = fadeIn() + slideInHorizontally(),
  78 ❌                 exit = fadeOut() + slideOutHorizontally()
  79                ) {
  80                    MainContent(
  81 ❌                     modifier = Modifier.padding(padding),
  82                        uiState = uiState,
  83                        currentQuery = currentQuery,
  84                        onQueryChange = { currentQuery = it },
  85                        onGenerateCommand = { 
  86                            if (currentQuery.isNotBlank()) {
  87                                viewModel.generateCommand(currentQuery)
  88                            }
  89                        },
  90                        onUpdateCommand = { newCommand ->
  91                            viewModel.updateCommand(newCommand)
  92                        },
  93                        onExecuteCommand = { command, isEdited ->
  94                            viewModel.executeCommand(command, currentQuery, isEdited)
  95                            currentQuery = ""
  96                        },
  97                        onCancelCommand = { viewModel.cancelCommand() },
  98                        onReset = { viewModel.reset() }
  99                    )
 100                }
 101                
 102 ❌             AnimatedVisibility(
 103                    visible = showHistory,
 104 ❌                 enter = fadeIn() + slideInHorizontally { it },
 105 ❌                 exit = fadeOut() + slideOutHorizontally { it }
 106                ) {
 107                    HistoryView(
 108 ❌                     modifier = Modifier.padding(padding),
 109                        history = commandHistory,
 110                        onSelectCommand = { item ->
 111                            currentQuery = item.query
 112                            showHistory = false
 113                        },
 114                        onClearHistory = { viewModel.clearHistory() }
 115                    )
 116                }
 117            }
 118        }
 119    }

 121 ❌ @Composable
 122    private fun ServerStatusChip(
 123        status: TermuxCommandViewModel.ServerStatus,
 124        onRefresh: () -> Unit
 125    ) {
 126        val (text, color) = when (status) {
 127 ❌         is TermuxCommandViewModel.ServerStatus.Online -> "Online" to Color.Green
 128 ❌         is TermuxCommandViewModel.ServerStatus.Offline -> "Offline" to Color.Red
 129            is TermuxCommandViewModel.ServerStatus.Checking -> "Checking..." to JarvisCyan
 130 ❌         is TermuxCommandViewModel.ServerStatus.Unknown -> "Unknown" to Color.Gray
 131        }
 132        
 133 ❌     AssistChip(
 134            onClick = onRefresh,
 135 ❌         label = { Text(text, fontSize = MaterialTheme.typography.bodySmall.fontSize) },
 136            leadingIcon = {
 137 ❌             Icon(
 138 ❌                 Icons.Default.Circle,
 139                    null,
 140 ❌                 modifier = Modifier.size(8.dp),
 141                    tint = color
 142                )
 143            },
 144 ❌         colors = AssistChipDefaults.assistChipColors(
 145                containerColor = color.copy(alpha = 0.2f)
 146            )
 147        )
 148    }
 149    
 150 ❌ @Composable
 151    private fun MainContent(
 152 ❌     modifier: Modifier = Modifier,
 153        uiState: TermuxCommandViewModel.LlamaUiState,
 154        currentQuery: String,
 155        onQueryChange: (String) -> Unit,
 156        onGenerateCommand: () -> Unit,
 157        onUpdateCommand: (String) -> Unit,
 158        onExecuteCommand: (String, Boolean) -> Unit,
 159        onCancelCommand: () -> Unit,
 160        onReset: () -> Unit
 161    ) {
 162 ❌     Column(
 163            modifier = modifier
 164                .fillMaxSize()
 165 ❌             .padding(16.dp)
 166        ) {
 167            QueryInputSection(
 168                query = currentQuery,
 169                onQueryChange = onQueryChange,
 170                onGenerate = onGenerateCommand,
 171                enabled = uiState is TermuxCommandViewModel.LlamaUiState.Idle
 172            )
 173            
 174 ❌         Spacer(modifier = Modifier.height(16.dp))
 175            
 176 ❌         Box(
 177 ❌             modifier = Modifier
 178                    .weight(1f)
 179                    .fillMaxWidth()
 180            ) {
 181                when (uiState) {
 182                    is TermuxCommandViewModel.LlamaUiState.Idle -> IdleState()
 183                    is TermuxCommandViewModel.LlamaUiState.CheckingServer -> CheckingServerState()
 184                    is TermuxCommandViewModel.LlamaUiState.Generating -> GeneratingState()
 185                    is TermuxCommandViewModel.LlamaUiState.WaitingConfirmation -> {
 186                        EditableConfirmationState(
 187                            command = uiState.command,
 188                            isEdited = uiState.isEdited,
 189                            onCommandChange = onUpdateCommand,
 190                            onExecute = { command: String, isEdited: Boolean -> 
 191                                onExecuteCommand(command, isEdited) 
 192                            },
 193                            onCancel = onCancelCommand
 194                        )
 195                    }
 196                    is TermuxCommandViewModel.LlamaUiState.Executing -> ExecutingState()
 197                    is TermuxCommandViewModel.LlamaUiState.Success -> {
 198                        SuccessState(
 199                            output = uiState.output,
 200                            exitCode = uiState.exitCode,
 201                            executionMethod = uiState.executionMethod,
 202                            onReset = onReset
 203                        )
 204                    }
 205                    is TermuxCommandViewModel.LlamaUiState.Error -> {
 206                        ErrorState(
 207                            message = uiState.message,
 208                            onRetry = onReset
 209                        )
 210                    }
 211                }
 212            }
 213        }
 214    }

 217    private fun QueryInputSection(
 218        query: String,
 219        onQueryChange: (String) -> Unit,
 220        onGenerate: () -> Unit,
 221        enabled: Boolean
 222    ) {
 223 ❌     Column {
 224 ❌         OutlinedTextField(
 225                value = query,
 226                onValueChange = onQueryChange,
 227 ❌             modifier = Modifier.fillMaxWidth(),
 228 ❌             label = { Text("What do you want to do in Termux?") },
 229 ❌             placeholder = { Text("e.g., update all packages, list files, check disk space") },
 230                enabled = enabled,
 231 ❌             colors = OutlinedTextFieldDefaults.colors(
 232                    focusedBorderColor = JarvisCyan,
 233                    unfocusedBorderColor = JarvisCyan.copy(alpha = 0.5f)
 234                )
 235            )
 236            
 237 ❌         Spacer(modifier = Modifier.height(8.dp))
 238            
 239 ❌         Button(
 240                onClick = onGenerate,
 241 ❌             modifier = Modifier.fillMaxWidth(),
 242                enabled = enabled && query.isNotBlank(),
 243 ❌             colors = ButtonDefaults.buttonColors(
 244                    containerColor = JarvisBlue
 245                )
 246            ) {
 247 ❌             Icon(Icons.Default.AutoAwesome, null)
 248 ❌             Spacer(modifier = Modifier.width(8.dp))
 249 ❌             Text("Generate Command")
 250            }
 251        }
 252    }

 314    /**
 315     * Editable confirmation state
 316     * Shows TextField for command editing
 317     */
 318 ❌ @Composable
 319    private fun EditableConfirmationState(
 320        command: String,
 321        isEdited: Boolean,
 322        onCommandChange: (String) -> Unit,
 323        onExecute: (String, Boolean) -> Unit,
 324        onCancel: () -> Unit
 325    ) {
 326 ❌     var editableCommand by remember { mutableStateOf(command) }
 327        
 328        // Update when command changes from ViewModel
 329 ❌     LaunchedEffect(command) {
 330            editableCommand = command
 331        }
 332        
 333 ❌     Card(
 334 ❌         modifier = Modifier.fillMaxWidth(),
 335 ❌         colors = CardDefaults.cardColors(
 336 ❌             containerColor = if (isEdited) Color(0xFF2D5016) else JarvisCyan.copy(alpha = 0.1f)
 337            ),
 338 ❌         border = CardDefaults.outlinedCardBorder().copy(
 339 ❌             brush = androidx.compose.ui.graphics.SolidColor(
 340 ❌                 if (isEdited) Color.Green else JarvisCyan
 341                )
 342            )
 343        ) {
 344 ❌         Column(modifier = Modifier.padding(16.dp)) {
 345 ❌             Row(verticalAlignment = Alignment.CenterVertically) {
 346 ❌                 Icon(
 347 ❌                     if (isEdited) Icons.Default.Edit else Icons.Default.Warning,
 348                        null,
 349 ❌                     tint = if (isEdited) Color.Green else Color.Yellow,
 350 ❌                     modifier = Modifier.size(24.dp)
 351                    )
 352 ❌                 Spacer(modifier = Modifier.width(8.dp))
 353 ❌                 Text(
 354                        if (isEdited) "Edited Command" else "Confirm Command Execution",
 355 ❌                     style = MaterialTheme.typography.titleMedium,
 356 ❌                     fontWeight = FontWeight.Bold,
 357 ❌                     color = if (isEdited) Color.Green else JarvisCyan
 358                    )
 359                }
 360                
 361 ❌             Spacer(modifier = Modifier.height(16.dp))
 362                
 363 ❌             Text(
 364                    if (isEdited) 
 365                        "You've edited the command. Review and execute when ready." 
 366                    else 
 367                        "Review the generated command and edit if needed before execution.",
 368 ❌                 style = MaterialTheme.typography.bodyMedium,
 369 ❌                 color = Color.White.copy(alpha = 0.7f)
 370                )
 371                
 372 ❌             Spacer(modifier = Modifier.height(16.dp))
 373                
 374 ❌             OutlinedTextField(
 375                    value = editableCommand,
 376                    onValueChange = { 
 377 ❌                     editableCommand = it
 378 ❌                     onCommandChange(it)
 379                    },
 380 ❌                 modifier = Modifier
 381                        .fillMaxWidth()
 382 ❌                     .clip(RoundedCornerShape(8.dp))
 383 ❌                     .background(Color.Black.copy(alpha = 0.3f)),
 384 ❌                 textStyle = MaterialTheme.typography.bodyMedium.copy(
 385 ❌                     fontFamily = FontFamily.Monospace,
 386 ❌                     color = if (isEdited) Color.Green else Color.White
 387                    ),
 388 ❌                 colors = OutlinedTextFieldDefaults.colors(
 389 ❌                     focusedBorderColor = if (isEdited) Color.Green else JarvisCyan,
 390 ❌                     unfocusedBorderColor = if (isEdited) Color.Green.copy(alpha = 0.5f) else JarvisCyan.copy(alpha = 0.5f)
 391                    )
 392                )
 393                
 394 ❌             Spacer(modifier = Modifier.height(16.dp))
 395                
 396 ❌             Row(
 397 ❌                 modifier = Modifier.fillMaxWidth(),
 398 ❌                 horizontalArrangement = Arrangement.spacedBy(8.dp)
 399                ) {
 400 ❌                 OutlinedButton(
 401                        onClick = onCancel,
 402 ❌                     modifier = Modifier.weight(1f),
 403 ❌                     colors = ButtonDefaults.outlinedButtonColors(
 404 ❌                         contentColor = Color.Red
 405                        )
 406                    ) {
 407 ❌                     Icon(Icons.Default.Close, null)
 408 ❌                     Spacer(modifier = Modifier.width(4.dp))
 409 ❌                     Text("Cancel")
 410                    }
 411                    
 412 ❌                 Button(
 413                        onClick = { onExecute(editableCommand, isEdited) },
 414 ❌                     modifier = Modifier.weight(1f),
 415 ❌                     colors = ButtonDefaults.buttonColors(
 416 ❌                         containerColor = Color.Green
 417                        )
 418                    ) {
 419 ❌                     Icon(Icons.Default.PlayArrow, null)
 420 ❌                     Spacer(modifier = Modifier.width(4.dp))
 421 ❌                     Text("Execute")
 422                    }
 423                }
 424            }
 425        }
 426    }

 449 ❌ @Composable
 450    private fun SuccessState(
 451        output: String,
 452        exitCode: Int,
 453        executionMethod: String?,
 454        onReset: () -> Unit
 455    ) {
 456 ❌     Card(
 457 ❌         modifier = Modifier.fillMaxWidth(),
 458 ❌         colors = CardDefaults.cardColors(
 459 ❌             containerColor = Color.Green.copy(alpha = 0.1f)
 460            ),
 461 ❌         border = CardDefaults.outlinedCardBorder().copy(
 462 ❌             brush = androidx.compose.ui.graphics.SolidColor(Color.Green)
 463            )
 464        ) {
 465 ❌         Column(modifier = Modifier.padding(16.dp)) {
 466 ❌             Row(verticalAlignment = Alignment.CenterVertically) {
 467 ❌                 Icon(
 468 ❌                     Icons.Default.CheckCircle,
 469                        null,
 470 ❌                     tint = Color.Green,
 471 ❌                     modifier = Modifier.size(24.dp)
 472                    )
 473 ❌                 Spacer(modifier = Modifier.width(8.dp))
 474 ❌                 Text(
 475                        "Command Executed Successfully",
 476 ❌                     style = MaterialTheme.typography.titleMedium,
 477 ❌                     fontWeight = FontWeight.Bold,
 478 ❌                     color = Color.Green
 479                    )

  13    /**
  14     * ViewModel for Termux Command Generator
  15     * 
  16     * TWO EXECUTION PATHS:
  17     * 1. Unedited commands → /execute endpoint (server-side execution)
  18     * 2. Edited commands → File-based executor (jarvis_executor.sh)
  19     */
  20    class TermuxCommandViewModel(private val context: Context) : ViewModel() {
  21        private val llamaClient = TermuxLlamaClient(context = context)
  22        private val tag = "TermuxCommandViewModel"
  23        
  24        private val _uiState = MutableStateFlow<LlamaUiState>(LlamaUiState.Idle)
  25        val uiState: StateFlow<LlamaUiState> = _uiState.asStateFlow()
  26        
  27        private val _commandHistory = MutableStateFlow<List<CommandHistoryItem>>(emptyList())
  28        val commandHistory: StateFlow<List<CommandHistoryItem>> = _commandHistory.asStateFlow()
  29        
  30        private val _serverStatus = MutableStateFlow<ServerStatus>(ServerStatus.Unknown)
  31        val serverStatus: StateFlow<ServerStatus> = _serverStatus.asStateFlow()
  32        
  33        sealed class LlamaUiState {
  34            object Idle : LlamaUiState()
  35            object CheckingServer : LlamaUiState()
  36            object Generating : LlamaUiState()
  37            data class WaitingConfirmation(
  38                val command: String,
  39                val isEdited: Boolean = false
  40            ) : LlamaUiState()
  41            object Executing : LlamaUiState()
  42            data class Success(
  43                val output: String,
  44                val exitCode: Int,
  45                val executionMethod: String? = null
  46            ) : LlamaUiState()
  47            data class Error(val message: String) : LlamaUiState()
  48        }
  49        
  50        sealed class ServerStatus {
  51            object Unknown : ServerStatus()
  52            object Checking : ServerStatus()
  53            object Online : ServerStatus()
  54            object Offline : ServerStatus()
  55        }
  56        
  57        data class CommandHistoryItem(
  58            val query: String,
  59            val command: String,
  60            val output: String?,
  61            val success: Boolean,
  62            val executionMethod: String? = null,
  63            val timestamp: Long = System.currentTimeMillis()
  64        )
  65        
  66        init {
  67            checkServerStatus()
  68        }
  69        
  70        fun checkServerStatus() {
  71 ❌         viewModelScope.launch {
  72                _serverStatus.value = ServerStatus.Checking
  73 ❌             val isHealthy = llamaClient.checkHealth()
  74                _serverStatus.value = if (isHealthy) ServerStatus.Online else ServerStatus.Offline
  75            }
  76        }
  77        
  78        fun generateCommand(query: String) {
  79 ❌         viewModelScope.launch {
  80                try {
  81                    _uiState.value = LlamaUiState.Generating
  82                    
  83 ❌                 val result = llamaClient.generateCommand(query)
  84                    
  85                    if (result.success && result.command != null) {
  86                        _uiState.value = LlamaUiState.WaitingConfirmation(
  87                            command = result.command,
  88                            isEdited = false
  89                        )
  90                    } else {
  91                        _uiState.value = LlamaUiState.Error(
  92                            result.error ?: "Failed to generate command"
  93                        )
  94                        
  95                        addToHistory(
  96                            CommandHistoryItem(
  97                                query = query,
  98                                command = "",
  99                                output = result.error,
 100                                success = false
 101                            )
 102                        )
 103                    }
 104                } catch (e: Exception) {
 105                    _uiState.value = LlamaUiState.Error(e.message ?: "Unknown error")
 106                }
 107            }
 108        }
 109        
 110        fun updateCommand(newCommand: String) {
 111            val currentState = _uiState.value
 112            if (currentState is LlamaUiState.WaitingConfirmation) {
 113                _uiState.value = LlamaUiState.WaitingConfirmation(
 114                    command = newCommand,
 115                    isEdited = true  // Mark as edited
 116                )
 117            }
 118        }
 119        
 120        /**
 121         * Execute command with TWO PATHS:
 122         * 
 123         * PATH 1 (isEdited = false): Unedited LLM-generated command
 124         *   → executeCommandViaServer() → /execute endpoint
 125         *   → Server executes in Termux and returns result
 126         * 
 127         * PATH 2 (isEdited = true): User-edited command
 128         *   → executeCommandViaFile() → jarvis_executor.sh
 129         *   → File-based execution with immediate feedback
 130         */
 131        fun executeCommand(command: String, query: String, isEdited: Boolean) {
 132 ❌         viewModelScope.launch {
 133                try {
 134                    _uiState.value = LlamaUiState.Executing
 135                    
 136                    val result = if (isEdited) {
 137                        // EDITED COMMAND → File-based executor
 138                        Log.d(tag, "Executing EDITED command via file-based executor")
 139 ❌                     llamaClient.executeCommandViaFile(command)
 140                    } else {
 141                        // UNEDITED COMMAND → Server /execute endpoint
 142                        Log.d(tag, "Executing UNEDITED command via server /execute")
 143 ❌                     llamaClient.executeCommandViaServer(command)
 144                    }
 145                    
 146                    if (result.success) {
 147                        _uiState.value = LlamaUiState.Success(
 148                            output = result.output ?: "Command completed",
 149                            exitCode = result.exitCode ?: 0,
 150                            executionMethod = result.method
 151                        )
 152                        
 153                        addToHistory(
 154                            CommandHistoryItem(
 155                                query = query,
 156                                command = command,
 157                                output = result.output,
 158                                success = true,
 159                                executionMethod = result.method
 160                            )
 161                        )
 162                    } else {
 163                        _uiState.value = LlamaUiState.Error(
 164                            result.error ?: "Execution failed"
 165                        )
 166                        
 167                        addToHistory(
 168                            CommandHistoryItem(
 169                                query = query,
 170                                command = command,
 171                                output = result.error,
 172                                success = false,
 173                                executionMethod = result.method
 174                            )
 175                        )
 176                    }
 177                } catch (e: Exception) {
 178                    Log.e(tag, "Execution error", e)
 179                    _uiState.value = LlamaUiState.Error(e.message ?: "Execution failed")
 180                }
 181            }
 182        }
 183        
 184        fun cancelCommand() {
 185            _uiState.value = LlamaUiState.Idle
 186        }
 187        
 188        fun reset() {
 189            _uiState.value = LlamaUiState.Idle
 190        }
 191        
 192        private fun addToHistory(item: CommandHistoryItem) {
 193            _commandHistory.value = listOf(item) + _commandHistory.value
 194        }
 195        
 196        fun clearHistory() {
 197            _commandHistory.value = emptyList()
 198        }
 199    }

 351    
 352    data class PairingResult(
 353        val success: Boolean,
 354        val deviceId: String? = null,
 355        val token: String? = null,
 356        val error: String? = null
 357    )
 358    
 359    suspend fun pairWithServer(context: Context, ip: String, port: String, deviceName: String): PairingResult {
 360        return withContext(Dispatchers.IO) {
 361            try {
 362                val url = URL("http://$ip:$port/api/v1/pair")
 363                val connection = url.openConnection() as HttpURLConnection
 364                
 365                connection.requestMethod = "POST"
 366                connection.setRequestProperty("Content-Type", "application/json")
 367                connection.doOutput = true
 368                connection.connectTimeout = 5000
 369                connection.readTimeout = 5000
 370                
 371                val jsonBody = """{"device_name":"$deviceName"}"""
 372                connection.outputStream.use { it.write(jsonBody.toByteArray()) }
 373                
 374                val responseCode = connection.responseCode
 375                val responseBody = if (responseCode == 200) {
 376                    connection.inputStream.bufferedReader().use { it.readText() }
 377                } else {
 378                    connection.errorStream?.bufferedReader()?.use { it.readText() } ?: "No response"
 379                }
 380                
 381                if (responseCode == 200) {
 382 ❌                 val json = Json { ignoreUnknownKeys = true }
 383                    val response = json.decodeFromString<PairResponse>(responseBody)
 384                    
 385                    if (response.success) {
 386                        PairingResult(
 387                            success = true,
 388                            deviceId = response.data?.device_id,
 389                            token = response.data?.token
 390                        )
 391                    } else {
 392                        PairingResult(success = false, error = response.error ?: "Unknown error")
 393                    }
 394                } else {
 395                    PairingResult(success = false, error = "HTTP $responseCode: $responseBody")
 396                }
 397            } catch (e: Exception) {
 398                Log.e("ClientPairing", "Pairing failed", e)
 399                PairingResult(success = false, error = e.message ?: "Connection failed")
 400            }
 401        }
 402    }
 403    
 404 ❌ @kotlinx.serialization.Serializable
 405    data class PairResponse(
 406        val success: Boolean,
 407        val data: PairData? = null,
 408        val error: String? = null
 409    )
 410    
 411 ❌ @kotlinx.serialization.Serializable
 412    data class PairData(
 413        val device_id: String,
 414        val token: String
 415    )

 127                            cloudProvider = if (cloudProvider == "OPENAI") "ANTHROPIC" else "OPENAI"
 128                            prefs.edit().putString("cloud_provider", cloudProvider).apply()
 129                        }
 130                    }
 131 ❌                 item {
 132                        SettingsTile("API Key", if (apiKey.isEmpty()) "Not Configured" else "••••••••",
 133 ❌                         Icons.Default.Key) { showApiKeyDialog = true }
 134                    }
 135    
 136                    // ── LAN Server ────────────────────────────────────────────
 137 ❌                 item { SectionLabel("LAN SERVER") }
 138 ❌                 item {
 139 ❌                     SettingsTile("Server IP", serverIpAddress, Icons.Default.Wifi) {}
 140                    }
 141 ❌                 item {
 142                        SettingsTile(
 143                            "Server Status",
 144                            if (isServerRunning) "Running on $serverIpAddress:8081" else "Stopped — tap to start",
 145 ❌                         if (isServerRunning) Icons.Default.CheckCircle else Icons.Default.Cancel
 146                        ) {
 147                            if (isServerRunning) {
 148                                LanServerService.stop(context)
 149                                isServerRunning = false
 150                            } else {
 151                                LanServerService.start(context)
 152                                isServerRunning = true
 153                                serverIpAddress = getLocalIpAddress()
 154                            }
 155                        }
 156                    }
 157 ❌                 item {
 158                        SettingsTile(
 159                            "Pairing Mode",
 160                            if (isPairingMode) "Active ($pairingTimeLeft sec remaining)" else "Disabled — tap to enable",
 161 ❌                         if (isPairingMode) Icons.Default.BluetoothSearching else Icons.Default.BluetoothDisabled
 162                        ) {
 163                            if (isPairingMode) {
 164                                securityManager.disablePairingMode()
 165                                isPairingMode = false
 166                            } else {
 167                                securityManager.enablePairingMode(60)
 168                                isPairingMode = true
 169                                showPairingDialog = true
 170                            }
 171                        }
 172                    }
 173 ❌                 item {
 174                        SettingsTile(
 175                            "Paired Devices",
 176                            "${pairedDevices.size} device(s) paired",
 177 ❌                         Icons.Default.Devices
 178                        ) {
 179                            pairedDevices = securityManager.getPairedDevices()
 180                            showDeviceListDialog = true
 181                        }
 182                    }
 183 ❌                 item {
 184                        SettingsTile(
 185                            "Pair As Client",
 186                            "Connect to another JARVIS device",
 187 ❌                         Icons.Default.DeviceHub
 188                        ) { showClientPairingScreen = true }
 189                    }
 190    
 191                    // ── Agent ─────────────────────────────────────────────────
 192 ❌                 item { SectionLabel("AGENT SETTINGS") }
 193 ❌                 item {
 194                        SettingsTileEditable(
 195                            title  = "Phone A Host",
 196                            value  = agentHost,
 197 ❌                         icon   = Icons.Default.PhoneAndroid,
 198                            hint   = "192.168.29.48",
 199                            onSave = { v ->
 200                                agentHost = v
 201 ❌                             JarvisPrefs.putString("agent_host", v)
 202                            }
 203                        )
 204                    }
 205    
 206                    // ── Voice / TTS ────────────────────────────────────────────
 207 ❌                 item { SectionLabel("VOICE — ELEVENLABS") }
 208 ❌                 item {
 209                        SettingsTileToggle(
 210                            title    = "Voice Output",
 211                            checked  = ttsEnabled,
 212 ❌                         icon     = Icons.Default.VolumeUp,
 213                            onToggle = { v ->
 214                                ttsEnabled = v
 215                                prefs.edit().putBoolean("tts_enabled", v).apply()
 216                            }
 217                        )
 218                    }
 219 ❌                 item {
 220                        SettingsTile(
 221                            "ElevenLabs API Keys",
 222                            if (elevenApiKeys.isBlank()) "Not configured"
 223 ❌                         else "${elevenApiKeys.lines().filter { it.isNotBlank() }.size} key(s) — one per line",
 224 ❌                         Icons.Default.Key
 225                        ) { showElevenDialog = true }
 226                    }
 227 ❌                 item {
 228 ❌                     SettingsTile("Voice", selectedVoice, Icons.Default.RecordVoiceOver) {
 229                            showVoiceDialog = true
 230                        }
 231                    }
 232                }
 233            }
 234        }
 235    
 236        // ── Dialogs ───────────────────────────────────────────────────────────────
 237    
 238        if (showModelDialog) {
 239            ModelSelectorDialog(
 240                selected  = selectedModel,
 241                onSelect  = { m ->
 242                    selectedModel = m
 243                    prefs.edit().putString("selected_model", m).apply()
 244                    showModelDialog = false
 245                },
 246                onDismiss = { showModelDialog = false }
 247            )
 248        }
 249    
 250        if (showApiKeyDialog) {
 251            ApiKeyDialog(
 252                current      = apiKey,
 253                showKey      = showApiKey,
 254                onToggleShow = { showApiKey = !showApiKey },
 255                onSave       = { k ->
 256                    apiKey = k
 257                    prefs.edit().putString("cloud_api_key", k).apply()
 258                    showApiKeyDialog = false
 259                },
 260                onDismiss = { showApiKeyDialog = false }
 261            )
 262        }
 263    
 264        if (showElevenDialog) {
 265            MultiLineDialog(
 266                title     = "ElevenLabs API Keys",
 267                hint      = "One key per line — all keys rotate on quota exhaustion",
 268                current   = elevenApiKeys,
 269                onSave    = { v ->
 270                    elevenApiKeys = v
 271                    prefs.edit().putString("elevenlabs_keys", v).apply()
 272                    showElevenDialog = false
 273                },
 274                onDismiss = { showElevenDialog = false }
 275            )
 276        }
 277    
 278        if (showVoiceDialog) {
 279            VoiceSelectorDialog(
 280                selected  = selectedVoice,
 281                voices    = VOICE_MAP.keys.toList(),
 282                onSelect  = { v ->
 283                    selectedVoice = v
 284                    prefs.edit().putString("elevenlabs_voice", v).apply()
 285                    VOICE_MAP[v]?.let { id ->
 286                        prefs.edit().putString("elevenlabs_voice_id", id).apply()
 287                    }
 288                    showVoiceDialog = false
 289                },
 290                onDismiss = { showVoiceDialog = false }
 291            )
 292        }
 293    
 294        if (showPairingDialog) {
 295            PairingInstructionsDialog(
 296                ipAddress = serverIpAddress,
 297                timeLeft  = pairingTimeLeft,
 298                onDismiss = { showPairingDialog = false }
 299            )
 300        }
 301    
 302        if (showDeviceListDialog) {
 303            DeviceListDialog(
 304                devices   = pairedDevices,
 305                onDismiss = { showDeviceListDialog = false },
 306                onUnpair  = { deviceId ->
 307                    securityManager.unpairDevice(deviceId)
 308                    pairedDevices = securityManager.getPairedDevices()
 309                }
 310            )
 311        }
 312    }

 655    private fun DeviceListDialog(

 659    ) {
 660 ❌     var deviceToUnpair by remember { mutableStateOf<String?>(null) }

 701 ❌         AlertDialog(

 704 ❌             text  = { Text("This device will lose access to this server.",

 722    private fun getModelDisplayName(model: String) = when (model) {
 723        "gpt-4o-mini"                       -> "GPT-4.1 Mini"
 724        "gpt-4o"                            -> "GPT-4.1"
 725        "o1-mini"                           -> "GPT-O1 Mini"
 726        "deepseek/deepseek-r1:free"         -> "DeepSeek R1 Free"
 727        "google/gemini-2.0-flash-lite:free" -> "Gemini 2.0 Flash"
 728        "local"                             -> "Local LLM"
 729        else                                -> model
 730    }
 731    
 732    private fun getLocalIpAddress(): String {
 733        return try {
 734            NetworkInterface.getNetworkInterfaces()?.toList()
 735                ?.flatMap { it.inetAddresses.toList() }
 736                ?.firstOrNull { !it.isLoopbackAddress && it.hostAddress?.contains(':') == false }
 737                ?.hostAddress ?: "Unknown"
 738        } catch (e: Exception) {
 739            "Unknown"
 740        }
 741    }

  40     * FloatingTimerService

  70            const val EXTRA_TASK_NAME     = "ft_task_name"
  71            const val EXTRA_TOTAL_SECONDS = "ft_total_seconds"
  72    
  73            fun canDrawOverlays(context: Context): Boolean =
  74                Build.VERSION.SDK_INT < Build.VERSION_CODES.M || Settings.canDrawOverlays(context)
  75    
  76            fun start(context: Context, taskId: String, taskName: String, totalSeconds: Long) {
  77                if (!canDrawOverlays(context)) return
  78                val intent = Intent(context, FloatingTimerService::class.java).apply {
  79                    putExtra(EXTRA_TASK_ID,       taskId)
  80                    putExtra(EXTRA_TASK_NAME,     taskName)
  81                    putExtra(EXTRA_TOTAL_SECONDS, totalSeconds)
  82                }
  83                if (Build.VERSION.SDK_INT >= Build.VERSION_CODES.O)
  84                    context.startForegroundService(intent)
  85                else
  86                    context.startService(intent)
  87            }
  88    
  89            fun stop(context: Context) {
  90                context.stopService(Intent(context, FloatingTimerService::class.java))
  91            }
  92        }

 121        }

 132    
 133            // Thin bar: match_parent width, wrap height, anchored to bottom
 134            val params = WindowManager.LayoutParams(

 137                overlayType,
 138                WindowManager.LayoutParams.FLAG_NOT_FOCUSABLE or

 151 ❌             setContent {

 174                }
 175            }

 318    /** Format seconds as "MM:SS" or "H:MM:SS" */
 319    private fun formatBarTime(seconds: Long): String {
 320        val h = seconds / 3600
 321        val m = (seconds % 3600) / 60
 322        val s = seconds % 60
 323        return if (h > 0) "%d:%02d:%02d".format(h, m, s)
 324        else              "%02d:%02d".format(m, s)
 325    }

  96    private fun formatTime(seconds: Long): String {
  97        val hours = seconds / 3600
  98        val minutes = (seconds % 3600) / 60
  99        val secs = seconds % 60
 100        
 101        return if (hours > 0) {
 102            String.format("%d:%02d:%02d", hours, minutes, secs)
 103        } else {
 104            String.format("%02d:%02d", minutes, secs)
 105        }

   1    package com.jarvismini.voice

   3    import android.content.Context

  14    import java.util.concurrent.TimeUnit
  15    
  16    /**
  17     * VoiceModule — Jarvis voice output
  18     *
  19     * Priority:
  20     *   1. ElevenLabs via agent_server TTS proxy (port 8892) — natural voice
  21     *   2. Android system TTS — fallback if server unreachable
  22     *
  23     * Multi-key rotation and voice selection are handled server-side.
  24     * This class just POSTs to the Termux TTS server.
  25     */
  26    object VoiceModule {
  27    
  28        private const val TAG       = "VoiceModule"
  29        private const val TTS_PORT  = 8892
  30        private const val DEFAULT_HOST = "192.168.29.48"
  31    
  32        private var systemTts: TextToSpeech? = null
  33        private var systemTtsReady = false
  34    
  35 ❌     private val http = OkHttpClient.Builder()
  36            .connectTimeout(3, TimeUnit.SECONDS)
  37            .readTimeout(30, TimeUnit.SECONDS)
  38            .build()
  39    
  40        fun init(context: Context) {
  41            systemTts = TextToSpeech(context) { status ->
  42                systemTtsReady = (status == TextToSpeech.SUCCESS)
  43                Log.d(TAG, "System TTS ready: $systemTtsReady")
  44            }
  45        }
  46    
  47        fun destroy() {
  48            systemTts?.stop()
  49            systemTts?.shutdown()
  50            systemTts = null
  51        }
  52    
  53        /**
  54         * Speak text. Tries ElevenLabs server first, falls back to Android TTS.
  55         */
  56        suspend fun speak(
  57            text:    String,
  58            voiceId: String? = null,
  59            mode:    SpeakMode = SpeakMode.AUTO
  60        ) {
  61            if (text.isBlank()) return
  62            when (mode) {
  63                SpeakMode.ELEVENLABS_ONLY -> speakElevenLabs(text, voiceId)
  64                SpeakMode.SYSTEM_ONLY     -> speakSystem(text)
  65                SpeakMode.AUTO -> {
  66                    val ok = speakElevenLabs(text, voiceId)
  67                    if (!ok) speakSystem(text)
  68                }
  69            }
  70        }
  71    
  72        private suspend fun speakElevenLabs(text: String, voiceId: String?): Boolean =
  73            withContext(Dispatchers.IO) {
  74                try {
  75 ❌                 val host = JarvisPrefs.getString("agent_host") ?: DEFAULT_HOST
  76                    val body = JSONObject().apply {
  77                        put("text", text)
  78                        if (voiceId != null) put("voice_id", voiceId)
  79 ❌                 }.toString().toRequestBody("application/json".toMediaType())
  80    
  81 ❌                 val req = Request.Builder()
  82                        .url("http://$host:$TTS_PORT/speak")
  83                        .post(body)
  84                        .build()
  85    
  86                    val resp = http.newCall(req).execute()
  87                    val ok   = resp.isSuccessful
  88                    resp.close()
  89                    Log.d(TAG, "ElevenLabs TTS: ${if (ok) "ok" else "failed"}")
  90                    ok
  91                } catch (e: Exception) {
  92                    Log.w(TAG, "ElevenLabs unreachable: ${e.message}")
  93                    false
  94                }
  95            }
  96    
  97        private fun speakSystem(text: String) {
  98            if (!systemTtsReady) {
  99                Log.w(TAG, "System TTS not ready")
 100                return
 101            }
 102            systemTts?.speak(text, TextToSpeech.QUEUE_FLUSH, null, "jarvis_${System.currentTimeMillis()}")
 103            Log.d(TAG, "System TTS: $text")
 104        }
 105    
 106        enum class SpeakMode { AUTO, ELEVENLABS_ONLY, SYSTEM_ONLY }
 107    }
